# Daily video prompt pack — 03/11/2026 · Snake day (ថ្ងៃឆ្នាំម្សាញ់)

Lucky signs: Snake, Ox, Rooster · Careful: Pig
Day element: Yin Metal ✨ (Xin-si day; ធាតុលោហៈ) · every action scene today carries Metal magic: bright silver-white sparkles, shimmering blades of light and softly chiming silver stars.
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.
Two versions: make the pictures once, then record a Khmer voice-over and an English voice-over (two videos, or one video with both subtitle tracks).
Human moments: in the love, work, money and horoscope scenes the golden magic opens a short vision of everyday Cambodian life with real feelings on real faces (the people never talk), and every scene with Chanda on camera says which feeling she shows.
Ratings, lucky signs and lucky numbers come from the site's fixed rules and match the website's daily fortune for this date.
Voice-over wording: standard script (this pack was made ahead; the site's own daily wording appears the day before).

## Today's horoscope – all 12 signs
Each sign's prediction for 03/11/2026: general luck, love, work, money and advice, in English and Khmer, ready to copy into your Facebook, TikTok, Telegram and YouTube posts. The videos speak the same predictions: the 60-second video (the Snake's luck and love), the 180-second video (love, work and money), the long videos (every sign) and the full-fortune video. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs, as in the videos.
Wording: the website's standard lines for each sign's rating (this date's written fortune is published on the website the day before; open the Studio at mybirthsign.com/studio/ then to use it).

One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color yellow (លឿង) · best time 01:00–03:00 · lucky numbers 9 · 85 · 208 · 9965 · 88927 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a photorealistic hero image of @Snake (the Snake guardian with their snake), the Snake of the Chinese zodiac, as a majestic celestial guardian from a high-budget fantasy epic. The snake symbolizes wisdom, intuition and quiet elegance: a wise, calm and elegant expression and posture that show this, mouth calm and closed. Realistic anatomy and textures, engraved gold ornaments with physically real metallic reflections. It stands among the giant roots of an ancient banyan tree beside a temple inspired by Angkor Wat, with glowing fireflies and pink lotus flowers on a still pool, under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky; moonbeams fall on the snake, golden particles and flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light swirl around it. Cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, volumetric mist, cinematic depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around it. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cartoon or plastic look, no cropped body parts.

## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Snake, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Snake as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Snake's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Snake and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune
   English: Born in the Year of the Snake? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់មែនទេ? នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។
2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
3. Personality
   English: If you were born in the Year of the Snake, people may describe you as wise, intuitive, and elegant. Today, that side of you shines.
   Khmer: បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន ប្រហែលជាស្គាល់អ្នកថា ឈ្លាសវៃ ចេះវិភាគ និងសមរម្យ។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ចំណុចល្អនោះកំពុងភ្លឺ។
4. Challenge
   English: Can you guess today's luckiest zodiac animal? Three clues: wise, intuitive, and elegant.
   Khmer: ទាយមើល៖ តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ? តម្រុយ៖ ឈ្លាសវៃ ចេះវិភាគ និងសមរម្យ។
5. Symbol

## Voice-over – 180-second video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Snake? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Tuesday, 3 November 2026, is a Snake day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Snake. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Wise and graceful, the Snake symbolizes wisdom, intuition, and quiet elegance. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Snake are wise, intuitive, and elegant. Snakes are deep thinkers who plan carefully before acting.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Snake? Your luck is strong today. Stay wise, calm and elegant. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Love and family today: A good day to reach out, share a meal and show your affection openly.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Work today: Customers and coworkers respond well to you. A good day to present your ideas and close a deal.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Money today: A favourable day for steady income. Save a small part of today's earnings before treating yourself.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is yellow. Wear something yellow, or carry a small yellow item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 1 to 3 in the early morning. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the northeast. When you start something new, face the northeast, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 9, 85, and 208. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 9, 85, 208!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Snake's lucky days of the week are Wednesday and Saturday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Snake can be secretive or prone to overthinking. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: yellow. Best time: from 1 to 3 in the early morning. Lucky direction: northeast. Lucky numbers: 9, 85, and 208.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

5

8

12

15

16

17

18

19
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,450

20

ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ទាក់ទងគ្នា ញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា

29

30

31

32

50

ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។

## Long YouTube video – all 12 signs (about 5 minutes)
A long YouTube video (5:10): today's luckiest animal, an overview, then all 12 signs in order (two 10-second clips each), then a short closing. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each sign's clips name its own character). Chanda opens the video on camera and narrates the rest off screen; the animals never speak. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 31.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Rat (ជូត) – Ordinary Day
1:00 Ox (ឆ្លូវ) – Good Day
1:20 Tiger (ខាល) – Ordinary Day
1:40 Rabbit (ថោះ) – Ordinary Day
2:00 Dragon (រោង) – Ordinary Day
2:20 Snake (ម្សាញ់) – Great Day
2:40 Horse (មមី) – Ordinary Day
3:00 Goat (មមែ) – Ordinary Day
3:20 Monkey (វក) – Ordinary Day
3:40 Rooster (រកា) – Good Day
4:00 Dog (ច) – Ordinary Day
4:20 Pig (កុរ) – Take It Easy
4:40 Closing

### Topic and audience
A full daily fortune for all twelve Chinese zodiac signs. For anyone who wants their own sign's rating, lucky color, best time and direction; chapters let each viewer jump to their sign.

### Thumbnail prompt – YouTube (16:9)
Make the picture in an image AI (for example Gemini or Flow) with Chanda's picture and the saved zodiac characters (named after their animals) added as references. Upload size: 16:9, at least 640 pixels wide (3840 x 2160 is best), JPG or PNG, under 2 MB if you upload from your phone.

With the words drawn in (English):
Eye-catching YouTube thumbnail, horizontal 16:9, 3840 x 2160. Photorealistic still from a high-budget fantasy epic blending Khmer royal heritage with a Chinese historical fantasy drama: real human faces with natural skin, lifelike zodiac animals in engraved ceremonial gold armor with real metallic reflections, very expressive faces and eyes. Background: a glowing Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under an enormous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, lotus pool, golden particles. Bright, high-contrast, rich colors (imperial gold, deep navy, ruby red) that stand out on a small phone screen; strong golden rim light separating the characters from the background; slightly blurred background so the faces pop. Big, simple composition with only two or three subjects. Keep the bottom-right corner free (YouTube puts the video length there). No watermark, no logo, no YouTube buttons or arrows, no cartoon or plastic look. The twelve golden-armored zodiac guardians in a tight arc across the frame, all looking at the camera with excited faces; three of them shine much brighter with golden auras, one rests calmly in a soft blue bubble of light. Large bold text, at most a few words, in thick rounded sans-serif capitals with a dark outline and a soft drop shadow, perfectly spelled: "ALL 12 SIGNS" and "TODAY". The text sits across the top of the frame in the dark sky, never over a face.

## Voice-over – long YouTube video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

## Story video – YouTube (about 5 minutes)
"The Golden Snake and the Moon Lantern" (ពស់មាស និងចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ): an original fairy tale (5:00) starring today's luckiest animal. A new story every day: today the golden snake and Phleu the firefly must bring the light back before the full-moon festival. Today's lucky color, direction, best time, lucky numbers and lucky days are part of the story. Make the picture of Phleu once (prompt below) and add it, Chanda and the Snake picture as ingredients in Flow. Chanda opens the story on camera and narrates the rest off screen; Phleu and the animals never speak (their words are told by Chanda). 30 clips of 10 seconds, in order 1 to 30.

### Character prompt – Phleu the firefly (make once, keep it)
Create a photorealistic image of Phleu, a tiny, lifelike magical glowing firefly with bright, expressive eyes, a soft golden-green glow, delicate translucent wings and a tiny pink-and-gold flower crown, as a small magical companion in a high-budget fantasy epic: realistic anatomy and textures with a soft warm magical glow, sitting on a glowing pink lotus by a still pond at night, a temple inspired by Angkor Wat and an enormous full moon behind, golden dust in the air. Cinematic lighting, beautiful depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, centered. No text, no letters, no watermark, no cartoon or plastic look.

Background: a glowing Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under an enormous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, lotus pool, golden particles. Big, simple composition with only two or three subjects. Keep the bottom-right corner free (YouTube puts the video length there). @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands on the moonlit temple terrace beside the lotus pond, facing the camera, smiling warmly and speaking directly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures, holding a glowing golden storybook as if about to begin a bedtime story. Behind her, @Snake (the Snake guardian with their snake) glows softly on its lotus pedestal; the zodiac characters look around curiously but do not speak. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward her face. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda; the snake is the saved Flow character @Snake. She looks truly delighted to see the viewer: a warm, happy smile, sparkling eyes and a small tilt of her head, like greeting a dear friend after a long time. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical.

She says in Khmer: "នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!" Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak.

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden snake bring the light back before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the moon lantern grew dim. Its light was almost gone. Without it, tonight's full-moon festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden snake. Today was a Snake day, and the snake felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A firefly named Phleu flew down. "Little Snake," she said, "only you can bring the light back. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Wise and graceful, the Snake symbolizes wisdom, intuition, and quiet elegance. Phleu smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To relight the moon lantern, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the snake, and it set off on its adventure, with Phleu right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Snake are wise, intuitive, and elegant. Snakes are deep thinkers who plan carefully before acting.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was yellow, the snake's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The snake tied a yellow ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Phleu, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the northeast, the snake's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But on the path stood an old, shaky bridge. The snake stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Snake can be secretive or prone to overthinking.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Phleu came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," she said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Step by step, the snake crossed the bridge. With every step, the planks glowed gold. On the other side, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Phleu. For the Snake today, that is from 1 to 3 in the early morning. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the snake rang the golden bells 9 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The snake climbed 85 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found a shining pearl of moonlight. It was the heart of the moon lantern!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the pearl, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Wednesday and Saturday shone brightest, the Snake's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the pearl carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Phleu right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The snake placed the pearl inside the moon lantern. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, whoosh! A golden light burst into the sky!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The lotus pond glowed again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The snake felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Snake, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: yellow. Best time: from 1 to 3 in the early morning. Lucky direction: northeast. Lucky numbers: 9, 85, and 208.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Phleu fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
ប៉ុន្តែព្រឹកនេះ ចង្កៀងនៃព្រះចន្ទ ស្រអាប់ទៅវិញ។ ពន្លឺរបស់វា ស្ទើរតែរលត់។ បើគ្មានវា ពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់យប់នេះ មិនអាចចាប់ផ្ដើមបានទេ។

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
នៅកណ្ដាលស្រះ ផ្កាឈូកមាសមួយបានរីក ហើយមានពស់មាសតូចមួយ ចេញមក។ ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ហើយពស់មានអារម្មណ៍ពិសេសណាស់។

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
សត្វអំពិលអំពែកមួយ ឈ្មោះភ្លឺ ហោះចុះមក។ ភ្លឺនិយាយថា "ពស់តូចអើយ មានតែអ្នកទេ ដែលអាចនាំពន្លឺត្រឡប់មកវិញ។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់អ្នក!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
ពស់ ឈ្លាសវៃ និងស្រស់ស្អាត។ ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់តំណាងឲ្យប្រាជ្ញា វិចារណញាណ និងភាពថ្លៃថ្នូរដ៏ស្ងប់ស្ងាត់។ ភ្លឺញញឹម។ នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
ដើម្បីបំភ្លឺចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទឡើងវិញ វីរបុរសតូច ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
ពស់និយាយថា "តោះទៅ!" ហើយវាក៏ចេញដំណើរផ្សងព្រេង ដោយមានភ្លឺ នៅក្បែរខ្លួនជានិច្ច។

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ជាមនុស្សឈ្លាសវៃ ចេះវិភាគ និងសមរម្យ។ ម្សាញ់គិតដិតដល់មុននឹងធ្វើសកម្មភាព។

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
រតនសម្បត្តិទីមួយ កំពុងរង់ចាំ នៅក្នុងសួនចង្កៀងមួយពាន់។ មានតែមួយប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលភ្លឺជាងគេ។ វាគឺពណ៌លឿង ដែលជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់ពស់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
បន្ទាប់មក ពួកគេមកដល់ផ្លូវបំបែកមួយ ដែលមានផ្លូវជាច្រើន។ ផ្លូវមួយ ចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។ វាចង្អុលទៅទិសឦសាន ដែលជាទិសសំណាងរបស់ពស់។

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
ប៉ុន្តែនៅលើផ្លូវនោះ មានស្ពានចាស់មួយ ដែលរង្គើ។ ពស់ឈប់។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ជួនកាលសម្ងាត់ខ្លួន ឬគិតច្រើនពេក។

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
ភ្លឺមកជិត ហើយញញឹម។ ភ្លឺនិយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។ ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។ អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។"

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
ម្ដងមួយជំហានៗ ពស់ឆ្លងកាត់ស្ពាន។ រាល់ជំហាន ក្ដារស្ពានភ្លឺពណ៌មាស។ នៅម្ខាងទៀត វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។ តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។ នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
ភ្លឺនិយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។" សម្រាប់ពស់ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី ទៀបភ្លឺ។ ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ពស់វាយកណ្ដឹងមាស ចំនួនប្រាំបួន ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។ ទីង! ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។ ពស់ឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួនប៉ែតសិបប្រាំ កាំ ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគុជពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទ ភ្លឺចែងចាំង។ វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
នៅជុំវិញគុជ មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។ ថ្ងៃពុធ និងថ្ងៃសៅរ៍ ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។ វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គុជយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានភ្លឺ នៅក្បែរ។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។ យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។ តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
ពស់ដាក់គុជចូលក្នុងចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ។ មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។ បន្ទាប់មក វឺត! ពន្លឺមាសមួយ បាញ់ឡើងទៅលើមេឃ!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ស្រះផ្កាឈូក ភ្លឺចែងចាំងម្ដងទៀត។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ ពស់មានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី ទៀបភ្លឺ។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខប្រាំបួន លេខប៉ែតសិបប្រាំ និងលេខពីររយប្រាំបី។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងភ្លឺ ក៏លង់លក់ ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

00:03:53,235 --> 00:03:55,930
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

91
00:03:55,980 --> 00:03:57,303
បន្ទាប់មក វឺត!

92
00:03:57,353 --> 00:03:59,950
ពន្លឺមាសមួយ បាញ់ឡើងទៅលើមេឃ!

93
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,491
ស្រះផ្កាឈូក ភ្លឺចែងចាំងម្ដងទៀត។

94
00:04:02,541 --> 00:04:03,639
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

95
00:04:03,689 --> 00:04:06,835
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

96
00:04:06,885 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

97
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,147
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

98
00:04:13,197 --> 00:04:15,032
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

99
00:04:15,082 --> 00:04:17,819
ពស់មានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

100
00:04:17,869 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

101
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:22,323
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ថ្ងៃនេះ

102
00:04:22,373 --> 00:04:24,018
ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

103
00:04:24,068 --> 00:04:26,645
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

104
00:04:26,695 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

105
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,657
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

106
00:04:31,707 --> 00:04:33,039
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។

107
00:04:33,089 --> 00:04:35,722
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

108
00:04:35,772 --> 00:04:37,186
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។

109
00:04:37,236 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 9 លេខ 85 និងលេខ 208។

110
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,107
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

111
00:04:44,157 --> 00:04:47,478
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងភ្លឺ ក៏លង់លក់

112
00:04:47,528 --> 00:04:49,950
ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

113
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

114
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

115
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

116
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

117
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-03-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,195
Can one little golden snake bring

2
00:00:02,245 --> 00:00:05,052
the light back before the full moon rises?

3
00:00:05,102 --> 00:00:07,909
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

4
00:00:07,959 --> 00:00:09,950
in Cambodia, our story begins.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,240
But this morning

6
00:00:11,290 --> 00:00:13,337
the moon lantern grew dim.

7
00:00:13,387 --> 00:00:15,434
Its light was almost gone.

8
00:00:15,484 --> 00:00:17,934
Without it, tonight's full-moon

9
00:00:17,984 --> 00:00:19,950
festival could not begin.

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:21,749
In the middle of the pond

11
00:00:21,799 --> 00:00:23,259
a golden lotus opened

12
00:00:23,309 --> 00:00:25,777
and out came a little golden snake.

13
00:00:25,827 --> 00:00:27,288
Today was a Snake day

14
00:00:27,338 --> 00:00:29,950
and the snake felt something special.

15
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,709
A firefly named Phleu flew down.

16
00:00:32,759 --> 00:00:34,778
"Little Snake," she said

17
00:00:34,828 --> 00:00:37,795
"only you can bring the light back.

18
00:00:37,845 --> 00:00:39,950
Today is your lucky day!"

19
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,882
Wise and graceful, the Snake symbolizes

20
00:00:42,932 --> 00:00:45,739
wisdom, intuition, and quiet elegance.

21
00:00:45,789 --> 00:00:46,717
Phleu smiled.

22
00:00:46,767 --> 00:00:48,221
That was exactly why

23
00:00:48,271 --> 00:00:49,950
the moon had chosen it.

24
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,110
To relight the moon lantern

25
00:00:52,160 --> 00:00:54,350
the little hero needed three

26
00:00:54,400 --> 00:00:56,910
lucky treasures: its lucky color

27
00:00:56,960 --> 00:00:59,950
its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

28
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,973
"Let's go!" said the snake

29
00:01:03,023 --> 00:01:06,578
and it set off on its adventure

30
00:01:06,628 --> 00:01:09,950
with Phleu right by its side.

31
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,807
People born in the Year of the Snake

32
00:01:12,857 --> 00:01:15,426
are wise, intuitive, and elegant.

33
00:01:15,476 --> 00:01:17,648
Snakes are deep thinkers who

34
00:01:17,698 --> 00:01:19,950
plan carefully before acting.

35
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,189
The first treasure was waiting

36
00:01:22,239 --> 00:01:24,801
in a garden of a thousand lanterns.

37
00:01:24,851 --> 00:01:26,666
Only one shone brightest.

38
00:01:26,716 --> 00:01:28,607
It was yellow, the snake's

39
00:01:28,657 --> 00:01:29,950
lucky color today.

40
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,933
The snake tied a yellow

41
00:01:31,983 --> 00:01:33,916
ribbon around its neck.

42
00:01:33,966 --> 00:01:36,933
"Wear your lucky color," said Phleu

43
00:01:36,983 --> 00:01:39,950
"and good luck stays close to you."

44
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,430
Next, they came to a crossroads

45
00:01:42,480 --> 00:01:43,710
with many paths.

46
00:01:43,760 --> 00:01:45,550
One path began to glow.

47
00:01:45,600 --> 00:01:47,710
It pointed to the northeast

48
00:01:47,760 --> 00:01:49,950
the snake's lucky direction.

49
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:51,369
But on the path stood

50
00:01:51,419 --> 00:01:52,788
an old, shaky bridge.

51
00:01:52,838 --> 00:01:54,004
The snake stopped.

52
00:01:54,054 --> 00:01:55,491
Sometimes, people born

53
00:01:55,541 --> 00:01:58,261
in the Year of the Snake can be secretive

54
00:01:58,311 --> 00:01:59,950
or prone to overthinking.

55
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:02,592
Phleu came close and smiled.

56
00:02:02,642 --> 00:02:05,139
"Breathe slowly," she said.

57
00:02:05,189 --> 00:02:06,837
"Trust your heart.

58
00:02:06,887 --> 00:02:09,950
You are stronger than you think."

59
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:11,851
Step by step, the snake

60
00:02:11,901 --> 00:02:13,421
crossed the bridge.

61
00:02:13,471 --> 00:02:16,727
With every step, the planks glowed gold.

62
00:02:16,777 --> 00:02:19,950
On the other side, it laughed with joy.

63
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,311
The little hero learned that its worries

64
00:02:23,361 --> 00:02:26,589
were small, and its good heart was big.

65
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950
Balance and courage always help us grow.

66
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347
At last, they reached a great

67
00:02:32,397 --> 00:02:34,413
stone door in the temple.

68
00:02:34,463 --> 00:02:36,562
Carved above it were bells

69
00:02:36,612 --> 00:02:39,950
stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

70
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,454
"The door opens only

71
00:02:41,504 --> 00:02:43,785
at your best time," said Phleu.

72
00:02:43,835 --> 00:02:45,213
For the Snake today

73
00:02:45,263 --> 00:02:48,296
that is from 1 to 3 in the early morning.

74
00:02:48,346 --> 00:02:49,950
They waited patiently.

75
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:51,502
When the time came

76
00:02:51,552 --> 00:02:54,864
the snake rang the golden bells 9 times

77
00:02:54,914 --> 00:02:56,847
its first lucky number.

78
00:02:56,897 --> 00:02:57,881
Ding!

79
00:02:57,931 --> 00:02:59,950
The door began to shine.

80
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:01,086
The door opened

81
00:03:01,136 --> 00:03:03,586
and a stairway of light appeared.

82
00:03:03,636 --> 00:03:06,162
The snake climbed 85 glowing steps

83
00:03:06,212 --> 00:03:07,905
its second lucky number

84
00:03:07,955 --> 00:03:09,950
up to the top of the tower.

85
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:12,250
At the top of the tower

86
00:03:12,300 --> 00:03:16,250
they found a shining pearl of moonlight.

87
00:03:16,300 --> 00:03:19,950
It was the heart of the moon lantern!

88
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,651
Around the pearl, seven little lights

89
00:03:22,701 --> 00:03:25,497
twinkled, one for each day of the week.

90
00:03:25,547 --> 00:03:28,271
Wednesday and Saturday shone brightest

91
00:03:28,321 --> 00:03:29,950
the Snake's lucky days.

92
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,989
Time was running out.

93
00:03:32,039 --> 00:03:34,610
Holding the pearl carefully

94
00:03:34,660 --> 00:03:37,329
the little hero hurried home

95
00:03:37,379 --> 00:03:39,950
with Phleu right beside it.

96
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting.

97
00:03:43,824 --> 00:03:45,538
The night was dark

98
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381
and the full moon was rising.

99
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950
Was it too late?

100
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:51,981
The snake placed the pearl

101
00:03:52,031 --> 00:03:53,856
inside the moon lantern.

102
00:03:53,906 --> 00:03:56,278
For a moment, nothing happened.

103
00:03:56,328 --> 00:03:57,294
Then, whoosh!

104
00:03:57,344 --> 00:03:59,950
A golden light burst into the sky!

105
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,104
The lotus pond glowed again.

106
00:04:02,154 --> 00:04:04,104
Lanterns rose into the sky

107
00:04:04,154 --> 00:04:06,642
and the full-moon festival began.

108
00:04:06,692 --> 00:04:07,873
Everyone cheered

109
00:04:07,923 --> 00:04:09,950
for the little golden hero!

110
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,669
The full moon shone down warmly

111
00:04:12,719 --> 00:04:14,687
as if to say thank you.

112
00:04:14,737 --> 00:04:17,669
The snake felt peaceful and proud.

113
00:04:17,719 --> 00:04:19,950
It had done its very best.

114
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,311
If you were born in the Year of the Snake

115
00:04:23,361 --> 00:04:25,114
today is your day too.

116
00:04:25,164 --> 00:04:27,737
Like our hero, be brave, be kind

117
00:04:27,787 --> 00:04:29,950
and follow your good heart.

118
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,817
Let's remember today's luck.

119
00:04:31,867 --> 00:04:33,150
Lucky color: yellow.

120
00:04:33,200 --> 00:04:33,950
Best time:

121
00:04:34,000 --> 00:04:36,150
from 1 to 3 in the early morning.

122
00:04:36,200 --> 00:04:37,950
Lucky direction: northeast.

123
00:04:38,000 --> 00:04:39,950
Lucky numbers: 9, 85, and 208.

124
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,188
And so, under the bright full moon

125
00:04:43,238 --> 00:04:46,712
the little golden hero and Phleu fell

126
00:04:46,762 --> 00:04:49,950
fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

127
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:51,617
That's your Chinese zodiac

128
00:04:51,667 --> 00:04:52,771
fortune for today.

129
00:04:52,821 --> 00:04:54,053
See all twelve signs

130
00:04:54,103 --> 00:04:56,745

131
00:04:56,795 --> 00:04:58,860
Follow us for tomorrow's fortune.

132
00:04:58,910 --> 00:04:59,950
Have a lucky day!

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Pig (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Pig, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Pig clashes with the Snake day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Pig as ingredients (the Pig is the only zodiac character in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the pig hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little paper crane of light keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Stop the scroll
   English: Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Pig? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.
   Khmer: ឈប់! កុំអូសរំលង។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ រាសីអ្នកថ្ងៃនេះមិនល្អទេ។ តែកុំបារម្ភ សូមមើលឲ្យចប់។
★ 2. Warning
   English: Year of the Pig, wait! Today is a clash day for you. Here's how to get through it calmly.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ចាំបន្តិច! ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃប៉ះឆុងសម្រាប់អ្នក។ មកមើលវិធីឆ្លងកាត់ថ្ងៃនេះដោយស្ងប់។
3. Question
   English: Does everything feel a bit off today? If you were born in the Year of the Pig, here's why.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ អ្វីៗហាក់ដូចជាមិនសូវស្រួលមែនទេ? បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ នេះជាមូលហេតុ។
4. Send it to a friend
   English: Know someone born in the Year of the Pig? Send this to them before they make a big decision today.
   Khmer: ស្គាល់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរទេ? ផ្ញើវីដេអូនេះទៅគាត់ មុនពេលគាត់សម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។
5. Only one sign
   English: Out of all twelve signs, only one should slow down today: the Pig.
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗថ្ងៃនេះ គឺឆ្នាំកុរ។

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)

Chanda is on camera and speaks to the viewer with natural lip movements that match her words. She says: "Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!" Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak.

## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Year of the Pig, wait! Today is a clash day for you. Here's how to get through it calmly.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 3 November, is a Snake day, opposite the Pig in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can be overly trusting or indulgent. Pause before you react. Be kind to yourself.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next. Small steps still count.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

## Long YouTube video – full fortune, 8:40 (mid-roll ads)
A full daily fortune for YouTube (8:40, 52 clips of 10 seconds), long enough for mid-roll ads. In order: the hook, the Great Day sign (Snake) in depth, the two Good Day signs (Ox and Rooster), the sign to take it easy (Pig) with its little story, the other eight signs, then a recap and goodbye. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients in Flow (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each part names its own character). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 4 and 52; in the others she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. Set the clip length to 10 seconds, make the clips in order 1 to 52, and paste the chapters into the YouTube description.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Great Day: Snake
2:40 Good Day: Ox
3:30 Good Day: Rooster
4:20 Take it easy: Pig
5:20 All the other signs
8:00 Recap and goodbye

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Every sign in one line
   English: One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានមួយឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ពីរឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ តើអ្នកជាឆ្នាំណា?
2. Before a big decision
   English: Before you make any big decision today, watch this: your lucky color, your best time, and whether to slow down.
   Khmer: មុននឹងសម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ពេលល្អ និងថាតើគួរធ្វើយឺតៗឬទេ។
3. Speak to the careful sign
   English: Born in the Year of the Pig? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរមែនទេ? សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ មុនធ្វើរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកផ្សេងទៀត សំណាងរបស់អ្នកក៏នៅទីនេះដែរ។
★ 4. Name the winner
   English: Today the Snake has its Great Day, and one sign should take it easy. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់មានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ហើយមានមួយឆ្នាំគួរប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ តើជាឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
5. Comment first
   English: Comment your sign now. By the end of this video, you'll know your lucky color, best time, and direction for today.
   Khmer: សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នកឥឡូវនេះ។ មុនវីដេអូនេះចប់ អ្នកនឹងដឹងពណ៌ ពេល និងទិសសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video today (the hook changes from day to day, and each one gives every viewer a reason to stay to find their own sign). To use another hook, replace the voice line in scene 1.

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

Make the picture in an image AI (for example Gemini or Flow) with Chanda's picture and the saved zodiac characters (named after their animals) added as references. Upload size: 16:9, at least 640 pixels wide (3840 x 2160 is best), JPG or PNG, under 2 MB if you upload from your phone.

## Voice-over – full fortune video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Today the Snake has its Great Day, and one sign should take it easy. Is it yours? A Yin Metal day!

Today is a Snake day. Coming up: one Great Day sign, two Good Days, one sign to take it easy, and all twelve.

Wise and graceful, the Snake symbolizes wisdom, intuition, and quiet elegance. Let it guide you.

ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ និងឆ្នាំរកា មានសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ ហើយឆ្នាំកុរ គួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ សូមរករាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងជំពូកខាងក្រោម។

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
មុននឹងចាប់ផ្ដើម សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយ subscribe ដើម្បីទទួលជោគជតារៀងរាល់ថ្ងៃ។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
ថ្ងៃអង្គារ ថ្ងៃទីបី ខែវិច្ឆិកា ជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់! តោះ មកមើលថា ថ្ងៃសំណាងនេះ នាំអ្វីមកឲ្យអ្នក។

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
ពស់ ឈ្លាសវៃ និងស្រស់ស្អាត។ ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់តំណាងឲ្យប្រាជ្ញា វិចារណញាណ និងភាពថ្លៃថ្នូរដ៏ស្ងប់ស្ងាត់។ សូមឲ្យវាដឹកនាំអ្នក។

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ជារឿយៗ ត្រូវបានគេស្គាល់ថា ឈ្លាសវៃ ចេះវិភាគ និងសមរម្យ។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់ ហើយរឿងល្អៗនឹងមកដល់។

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
ស្នេហា និងគ្រួសារថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ទាក់ទងគ្នា ញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា និងបង្ហាញក្ដីស្រឡាញ់ដោយបើកចំហ។ ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
ការងារថ្ងៃនេះ៖ អតិថិជន និងមិត្តរួមការងារ ឆ្លើយតបល្អចំពោះអ្នក។ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់បង្ហាញគំនិត និងបិទកិច្ចព្រមព្រៀង។

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
លុយកាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ចំណូលនឹងនរ។ សូមសន្សំប្រាក់ចំណូលថ្ងៃនេះមួយចំណែកតូច មុននឹងចាយលើខ្លួនឯង។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
ពណ៌សំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌លឿង។ កន្សែង កាបូប ឬខ្សែដៃពណ៌លឿង នាំសំណាងមកជិតអ្នក។ សូមឲ្យវារំលឹកអ្នក ឲ្យញញឹម និងមានទំនុកចិត្ត។

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
ពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី ទៀបភ្លឺ ហើយទិសសំណាង គឺទិសឦសាន។ សូមរៀបចំផែនការតាមនោះ។

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
លេខសំណាង៖ ប្រាំបួន ប៉ែតសិបប្រាំ និងពីររយប្រាំបី។ សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយរក្សាទុកវីដេអូនេះ!

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
ថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ គឺថ្ងៃពុធ និងថ្ងៃសៅរ៍។ សូមទុកសម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
រំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ជួនកាលសម្ងាត់ខ្លួន ឬគិតច្រើនពេក។ សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព។ សូមមើលបន្ត មានដំណឹងល្អៗទៀត។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ! ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។ ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។ ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌លឿង ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោងប្រាំបួន ដល់ម៉ោងដប់មួយ ព្រឹក។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។ លេខសំណាង៖ បួន ដប់បួន និងមួយរយដប់បួន។ សូមមើលបន្ត មានដំណឹងល្អៗទៀត។

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។ សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។ ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំរកា៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ! ឆ្នាំរកា ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។ ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌មាស ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី ទៀបភ្លឺ។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសឦសាន។ លេខសំណាង៖ ប្រាំ ប៉ែតសិបប្រាំពីរ និងប្រាំរយសាមសិប។ សូមមើលបន្ត មានដំណឹងល្អៗទៀត។

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។ សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃទីបី ខែវិច្ឆិកា ជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ទល់មុខឆ្នាំកុរ ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។ មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដូចការគ្រោងទុកទេ។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
ហើយវាមិនអីទេ។ មនុស្សគ្រប់គ្នា ធ្លាប់មានថ្ងៃបែបនេះ។ ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ គឺជាថ្ងៃសម្រាប់ដកដង្ហើមវែងៗ។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជួនកាលជឿគេពេក ឬបណ្ដោយចិត្តខ្លួនឯងពេក។ សូមឈប់បន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។ សូមមេត្តាខ្លួនឯង។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់ និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។ ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

Scene 31 (5:00–5:10)
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់ សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។ ជំហានតូចៗ ក៏មានតម្លៃដែរ។

Scene 32 (5:10–5:20)
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។ រាល់ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ធ្វើឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែរឹងមាំ ហើយថ្ងៃភ្លឺស្វាង កំពុងមកដល់។

Scene 33 (5:20–5:30)
ឆ្នាំជូត៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខៀវ។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំបួន ព្រឹក។ ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

Scene 34 (5:30–5:40)
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។ លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។ ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

Scene 35 (5:40–5:50)
ឆ្នាំខាល៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ទឹកក្រូច។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងដប់មួយ ព្រឹក ដល់ម៉ោងមួយ រសៀល។

Scene 36 (5:50–6:00)
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងត្បូង។ ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ ជាថ្ងៃនៃភាពច្បាស់លាស់។

Scene 37 (6:00–6:10)
ឆ្នាំថោះ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ផ្កាឈូក។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី រសៀល។ ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

Scene 38 (6:10–6:20)
ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលសាមញ្ញៗជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ ដូចជាដើរលេង ឬញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 39 (6:20–6:30)
ឆ្នាំរោង៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ប្រាក់។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងដប់មួយ យប់ ដល់ម៉ោងមួយ ទៀបភ្លឺ។

ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

69
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:13,006
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

70
00:03:13,056 --> 00:03:16,200
លេខសំណាង៖ 4 14 និង 114។

71
00:03:16,250 --> 00:03:19,950
សូមមើលបន្ត មានដំណឹងល្អៗទៀត។

72
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,610
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ថ្ងៃនេះ

73
00:03:22,660 --> 00:03:25,482
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។

74
00:03:25,532 --> 00:03:28,567
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

75
00:03:28,617 --> 00:03:29,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

76
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:32,746
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំរកា៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ!

77
00:03:32,796 --> 00:03:36,079
ឆ្នាំរកា ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង

78
00:03:36,129 --> 00:03:37,907
ជាមួយឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។

79
00:03:37,957 --> 00:03:39,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

80
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:42,298
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស

81
00:03:42,348 --> 00:03:44,037
និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។

82
00:03:44,087 --> 00:03:45,080
ស្នេហា៖

83
00:03:45,130 --> 00:03:48,472
សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។

84
00:03:48,522 --> 00:03:49,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

85
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:52,112
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌មាស

86
00:03:52,162 --> 00:03:57,653
ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

87
00:03:57,703 --> 00:03:59,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

88
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,597
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសឦសាន។

89
00:04:02,647 --> 00:04:05,979
លេខសំណាង៖ 5 87 និង 530។

90
00:04:06,029 --> 00:04:09,950
សូមមើលបន្ត មានដំណឹងល្អៗទៀត។

91

អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា ថ្ងៃនេះ

92
00:04:12,347 --> 00:04:15,052
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។

93
00:04:15,102 --> 00:04:18,011
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

94
00:04:18,061 --> 00:04:19,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

95

ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃទី 3 ខែវិច្ឆិកា ជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំម្សាញ់

96
00:04:23,982 --> 00:04:26,676
ទល់មុខឆ្នាំកុរ ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។

97
00:04:26,726 --> 00:04:29,950
មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដូចការគ្រោងទុកទេ។

98
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,090
ហើយវាមិនអីទេ។

99
00:04:31,140 --> 00:04:34,248
មនុស្សគ្រប់គ្នា ធ្លាប់មានថ្ងៃបែបនេះ។

100
00:04:34,298 --> 00:04:37,494
ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ

101
00:04:37,544 --> 00:04:39,950
គឺជាថ្ងៃសម្រាប់ដកដង្ហើមវែងៗ។

102
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,250
សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជួនកាលជឿគេពេក

103
00:04:43,300 --> 00:04:45,550
ឬបណ្ដោយចិត្តខ្លួនឯងពេក។

104
00:04:45,600 --> 00:04:48,250
សូមឈប់បន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។

105
00:04:48,300 --> 00:04:49,950
សូមមេត្តាខ្លួនឯង។

106

សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់

107
00:04:54,444 --> 00:04:56,370
និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។

108
00:04:56,420 --> 00:04:59,950
ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

109
00:05:00,000 --> 00:05:01,897
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់

110
00:05:01,947 --> 00:05:04,817
សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។

111
00:05:04,867 --> 00:05:07,826
បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។

112
00:05:07,876 --> 00:05:09,950
ជំហានតូចៗ ក៏មានតម្លៃដែរ។

113
00:05:10,000 --> 00:05:12,705
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។

114
00:05:12,755 --> 00:05:17,093
រាល់ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ធ្វើឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែរឹងមាំ

115
00:05:17,143 --> 00:05:19,950
ហើយថ្ងៃភ្លឺស្វាង កំពុងមកដល់។

116
00:05:20,000 --> 00:05:22,511
ឆ្នាំជូត៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

117
00:05:22,561 --> 00:05:24,584
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខៀវ។

118
00:05:24,634 --> 00:05:28,365
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 7 ដល់ម៉ោង 9 ព្រឹក។

119
00:05:28,415 --> 00:05:29,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

120
00:05:30,000 --> 00:05:33,418
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

121
00:05:33,468 --> 00:05:37,127
លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

122
00:05:37,177 --> 00:05:38,902
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

123
00:05:38,952 --> 00:05:39,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

124
00:05:40,000 --> 00:05:42,575
ឆ្នាំខាល៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

125
00:05:42,625 --> 00:05:45,325
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ទឹកក្រូច។

126
00:05:45,375 --> 00:05:49,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក ដល់ម៉ោង 1 រសៀល។

127
00:05:50,000 --> 00:05:53,895
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

128
00:05:53,945 --> 00:05:55,913
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងត្បូង។

129
00:05:55,963 --> 00:05:59,950
ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ ជាថ្ងៃនៃភាពច្បាស់លាស់។

130
00:06:00,000 --> 00:06:02,421
ឆ្នាំថោះ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

131
00:06:02,471 --> 00:06:04,891
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ផ្កាឈូក។

132
00:06:04,941 --> 00:06:08,421
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 រសៀល។

133
00:06:08,471 --> 00:06:09,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

134
00:06:10,000 --> 00:06:10,942
ស្នេហា៖

135
00:06:10,992 --> 00:06:14,165
សូមចំណាយពេលសាមញ្ញៗជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ

136
00:06:14,215 --> 00:06:16,810
ដូចជាដើរលេង ឬញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា។

137
00:06:16,860 --> 00:06:18,380
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។

138
00:06:18,430 --> 00:06:19,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

139
00:06:20,000 --> 00:06:22,608
ឆ្នាំរោង៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

140
00:06:22,658 --> 00:06:25,140
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ប្រាក់។

141

ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 11 យប់ ដល់ម៉ោង 1 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

142
00:06:30,000 --> 00:06:34,728
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

143

ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងជើង។

144

សូមមើលបន្ត មានដំណឹងល្អៗទៀត។

145
00:06:40,000 --> 00:06:42,336
ឆ្នាំមមី៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

146

147
00:06:44,318 --> 00:06:48,018
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 3 ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

148

149
00:06:50,000 --> 00:06:53,363
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

150

151

ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។

152
00:06:58,492 --> 00:06:59,950

153
00:07:00,000 --> 00:07:02,336
ឆ្នាំមមែ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

154
00:07:02,386 --> 00:07:04,495

155
00:07:04,545 --> 00:07:08,018
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

156
00:07:08,068 --> 00:07:09,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

157
00:07:10,000 --> 00:07:10,950
ស្នេហា៖

158

159
00:07:14,250 --> 00:07:16,867
ដូចជាដើរលេង ឬញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា។

160
00:07:16,917 --> 00:07:18,533
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងកើត។

161
00:07:18,583 --> 00:07:19,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

162

ឆ្នាំវក៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

163
00:07:22,667 --> 00:07:24,883
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខៀវ។

164
00:07:24,933 --> 00:07:29,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 11 យប់ ដល់ម៉ោង 1 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

165
00:07:30,000 --> 00:07:33,363
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

166
00:07:33,413 --> 00:07:37,013
លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

167
00:07:37,063 --> 00:07:38,601
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងជើង។

168
00:07:38,651 --> 00:07:39,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

169
00:07:40,000 --> 00:07:42,134
ឆ្នាំច៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

170
00:07:42,184 --> 00:07:44,203

171
00:07:44,253 --> 00:07:47,996

172

សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

173

ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

176

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes the voice fresh for every clip, so the voice text in every scene is written to describe exactly the same voice: the same words, in the same order, at the start of the audio line, for every scene and every video. To keep it that way:
- Always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Do not retype, shorten or translate the voice text, and do not add other words about the voice.
- Make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, with the same model and settings for every clip.
- Use the same language for the whole video (all English or all Khmer).
- Listen to each clip as it is made. If one sounds different, make only that clip again with the same copied prompt.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Photorealistic live-action fantasy film, the look of a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with the grandeur of a Chinese historical fantasy drama. Real human faces with natural skin texture, the zodiac characters saved in Flow, each named after its animal (@Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog, @Pig): a guardian warrior in ornate armor themed on the animal, with a lifelike animal beside them (realistic anatomy, real fur, feathers or scales), always exactly as in the saved character picture, noble and powerful (never cute toys or cartoons). Setting: a majestic Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with towering carved stone, an enormous luminous full moon, deep indigo starry sky, drifting mist, a still reflecting pool with floating pink lotus flowers and ornate golden lanterns. A living, magical sky in every shot: thin silver clouds drift slowly across the moon, stars twinkle, a soft halo pulses around the moon and sky lanterns rise gently in the distance. Action and magic in the style of a Chinese fantasy (xianxia) drama: graceful wire-fu flying leaps, slow-motion martial-arts moves, flowing robes and capes, glowing sword energy and swirling qi light, always without blood, injury or fighting between characters. Golden magic: shimmering gold particles, flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light and floating gold dust that never hide a face. Physically real lighting: cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, soft lantern glow, volumetric light rays; colors deep navy, imperial gold, ruby red and lotus pink. Cinematic depth of field, smooth slow camera movement, high-end visual effects, 4K detail. The zodiac guardians and their animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos, no cartoon or plastic look, no low-quality CGI.

## Scene 1 – Snake day (Chanda on camera)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. She looks truly delighted to see the viewer: a warm, happy smile, sparkling eyes and a small tilt of her head, like greeting a dear friend after a long time. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda. Then: magical and powerful. @Snake (the Snake guardian with their snake) stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it wraps protectively around its guardian, who rises into the air in a slow, graceful turn while glowing silver scales of light swirl around them like a shield, as today's Metal magic swirls around them: bright silver-white sparkles, shimmering blades of light and softly chiming silver stars. The lotus pond ripples with golden light, and a shower of tiny stars falls over the water. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. @Snake (the Snake guardian with their snake), @Ox (the Ox guardian with their ox) and @Rooster (the Rooster guardian with their rooster) fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, thousands of fireflies burst up from the reeds and circle them like stars. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Pig, take it easy
Photorealistic golden fantasy-epic style, same saved Flow characters (Chanda and the zodiac characters named after their animals). @Pig (the Pig guardian with their pig) rests on a lotus pedestal by the pond: it curls up calmly as glowing butterflies circle it, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The pig is the saved Flow character @Pig.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. Long golden ribbons of light unfurl from the temple towers and wave across the starry sky, and the huge full moon glows over the pond. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with the twelve golden-armored zodiac guardians around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)

ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។ ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ និងឆ្នាំរកា មានសំណាងល្អជាងគេនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ ចំណែកឆ្នាំកុរ គួរសម្រាកឲ្យស្រួលខ្លួន ធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ និងកុំប្រញាប់ក្នុងការសម្រេចចិត្ត។
សម្រាប់លេខសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ប៉ែតសិបប្រាំ និងពីររយប្រាំបី។ ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវដប់បួន និងមួយរយដប់បួន។ ឆ្នាំរកាប៉ែតសិបប្រាំពីរ និងប្រាំរយសាមសិប។ សូមសរសេរលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក នៅក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយនិយាយម្ដងទៀត!
ចូលមើលលេខសំណាង និងលម្អិតសម្រាប់រាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ នៅ mybirthsign.com
